Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The anatomy operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 984. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 970 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_984(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 984. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^970(S, F) is trivial. QED.
